Rather than adapting public issue text, every task is reverse-engineered from a real commit, pull request, or business scenario and rewritten as a short, colloquial, role-played request, so that a task's prompt is not recoverable by web-searching the underlying issue, pull request, or commit thread. Because the dataset is released openly - task directories, environment images, evaluation harness, tests, and reference solutions - contamination resistance rests on this construction together with dataset versioning rather than on secrecy. The four subsets - repository-level engineering, front-end development, office and business workflows, and red-/blue-team security - probe complementary facets of real work, each with its own verification style. All are packaged in a uniform task-directory format and run, under a uniform and reproducible protocol, on two agent harnesses (CodeBuddy Code and Claude Code); the full open release makes the benchmark reproducible end to end and directly auditable, since any third party can re-run each task and inspect its content. Because each subset uses a different scoring instrument, scores are not comparable across subsets and the suite reports no suite-wide average. We report a cross-model leaderboard across several model families.

</details>


### [144] [From a Word-Level Dictionary to Sentence-Level Semantics: Multilingual Grievance Labelling with Contextual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20946)
*Lin Tian,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现基于词汇匹配的 grievance 检测存在循环评估问题（词典将数据分为正负两类，导致 AUC 被构造性锁定在 0.5），并提出用上下文阅读模型替代词项匹配，在非循环基准上五语言评估显示阅读全文可将词典负类文本的平均精度从 0.14 提升至 0.20，尤其在引用、隐含和跨句 grievance 上提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 grievance 检测多基于词级词典（如 Grievance Dictionary）进行词项匹配，但该方法无法区分词项是被断言、引用、否定还是谴责；同时这些词典的评估常基于其自身检索到的样本，导致评估循环（高 AUC 部分反映的是词典自身选择规则的一致性而非真实检测能力）。

Method: 保留 Grievance Dictionary 的 22 个构念本体，但用上下文阅读模型替代词项匹配；构建非循环基准数据集（五语言，2000 条目），区分无条件随机、词典正类、词典负类三个层级；评估阅读目标句 vs. 阅读全文的效果差异。

Result: 原词典的宏 AUROC 为 0.686，但因评估数据被词典自身完美分割（所有"随机"标签条目实际均为词典负类），其有效 AUROC 仅为构造性下限 0.5。上下文阅读模型在词典负类文本上将平均精度从 0.14 提升至 0.20，最大增益来自引用、隐含和跨句 grievance。

Conclusion: 阅读上下文语境能更忠实地测量 grievance，而测试应使用词典未参与选择的数据才能获得更诚实的评估结果。

Abstract: Grievance is one of the warning signs analysts look for when assessing threats of violence. It is increasingly measured at scale from online text, most often with word-level lexicons like the Grievance Dictionary that score by matching weighted terms. Such matching is a fast and transparent proxy, but it cannot resolve whether a term is asserted, quoted, negated, or condemned. These lexicons are also often evaluated on pools enriched with the very examples they retrieve, so a high score partly reflects agreement with the lexicon's own selection rule. Examining a five-language, 2{,}000-item evaluation pool, we find its halves separated almost perfectly by the lexicon itself: every item labeled ``random'' is in fact lexicon-negative, so the lexicon's apparent macro-AUROC of 0.686 collapses to a 0.500 floor fixed by construction. We keep the dictionary's 22-construct ontology but replace term matching with context-reading models, evaluated on a non-circular benchmark that separates unconditional-random, lexicon-positive, and lexicon-negative strata across five languages. Reading the full post rather than the target sentence alone helps most where the lexicon is silent, raising average precision on lexicon-negative text from 0.14 to 0.20, with the largest gains on quoted, implicit, and cross-sentence grievance. Together, these results show that grievance is measured more faithfully by reading the surrounding context, and more honestly when tested on text the lexicon did not select. We release our code and benchmark at https://github.com/behavioral-ds/multilingual_grievance.

</details>


### [145] [Where Animacy Lives in Large Language Models: Tracing the Circuits of the Animacy Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20995)
*Samuele Punzo,Giovanni Cinà,Sandro Pezzelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型中存在处理有生性（animacy）的因果回路，该回路虽不如其他已知回路局部化，但确实存在，且在不同模型和任务间仅部分泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs能否区分书面语言中有生与无生概念，以及这种能力是否可追溯至模型中一组局部化的因果相关组件和连接。

Method: 构建最小对控制数据集，对四个开源模型进行回路发现（circuit discovery），并通过深入实验和消融分析验证因果机制的存在。

Result: 发现了处理有生性的因果回路（animacy circuit），但该回路相比其他已知回路局部化程度较低，且在不同模型和有生性任务间仅表现出部分泛化能力。

Conclusion: 有生性概念在大语言模型中呈现分布性、上下文依赖性和一定程度的梯度性，而非高度集中编码。

Abstract: Distinguishing animate from inanimate concepts in written language requires more than shallow text processing, as it involves recognizing complex selectional constraints and contextual cues, such as verb-argument interactions. Yet, current large language models (LLMs) appear to be capable of doing it. We investigate whether this animacy-sensitive behavior of LLMs can be traced to a localized set of causally relevant components and connections. To do so, we construct a controlled dataset of minimal pairs and perform circuit discovery on four open-weight models. Through in-depth experiments and ablations, we show that a causal mechanism responsible for handling animacy in these models does exist, thus discovering an animacy circuit. At the same time, this circuit appears to be less localized compared to other known ones and generalizes only partially across models and animacy tasks, confirming the distributed, context-dependent, and somewhat graded nature of the animacy concept.

</details>


### [146] [CultureTalk-ID: A Multi-Task Dialogue Benchmark for Cultural Commonsense in Indonesian Local Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21016)
*Muhammad Dehan Al Kautsar,Salsabila Pranida,Bilal Elbouardi,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CultureTalk-ID，首个基于对话的文化常识基准，涵盖印尼及当地语言的11种语言、13个文化主题的4,496个对话，通过三项互补任务（对话式文化常识推理、文化忠实机器翻译、语言引导）来评估LLM在对话语境中的文化理解、迁移和生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印尼文化常识基准使用简短孤立的提示，忽略了文化在对话中实际浮现的交互语境，无法有效评估LLM在真实对话场景中的文化理解能力。

Method: 通过多阶段人工流水线，由母语者构建包含11种语言、13个文化主题的4,496个文化对话，并设计了三个互补任务：对话式多项选择文化常识推理、文化忠实机器翻译、语言引导。

Result: 构建了首个基于对话的印尼文化常识基准，包含多样化的语言和文化主题对话数据，以及三项评估任务。

Conclusion: CultureTalk-ID通过对话式基准填补了现有印尼文化常识评估缺乏对话语境的空白，能够更全面地评估LLM在文化理解、迁移和生成方面的能力。

Abstract: Culture is lived through conversation, yet existing Indonesian cultural commonsense benchmarks evaluate LLMs on short and isolated prompts, stripping away the dialogic context in which cultural nuances actually surface. We introduce CultureTalk-ID, the first dialogue-based benchmark for cultural commonsense in Indonesian and its local languages, comprising 4,496 culturally grounded dialogues across 11 languages and 13 culturally salient topics, curated through a multi-stage human pipeline with native speakers to ensure authenticity. CultureTalk-ID introduces three complementary tasks, namely dialogue-based multiple-choice cultural commonsense reasoning, culturally faithful machine translation, and language steering, which jointly probe whether LLMs can understand, transfer, and generate culturally grounded language.

</details>


### [147] [Sample-Efficient Learning from Agent Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21051)
*Chenhui Gou,Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Jianfei Cai,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Experience Distillation方法，通过将智能体的交互历史经验蒸馏到模型权重中，在无需额外环境交互的情况下保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益，相比直接监督微调（仅保留3.8%）和传统强化学习基线（样本效率提升9.6倍以上）均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的智能体学习常受限于高成本的环境交互（如耗时实验或人工反馈）。上下文学习虽能高效利用交互历史，但经验一旦移出上下文其收益即消失；而上下文蒸馏虽能将信息内化到模型权重中，但如何在无需牺牲环境样本效率的前提下将其应用于智能体的交互历史仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Experience Distillation方法，在无需额外环境交互的情况下，将智能体收集到的交互历史经验蒸馏到模型权重中。在749个软件工程任务和6个文字冒险游戏上进行了实验验证。

Result: Experience Distillation在两个领域保留了至少64.8%的上下文学习收益；而直接监督微调仅恢复3.8%。与传统强化学习基线相比，上下文学习结合Experience Distillation在匹配其性能的同时，样本效率提升至少9.6倍。

Conclusion: Experience Distillation是一种高效的经验内化方法，能够在不增加环境交互成本的前提下显著保留上下文学习的收益，并大幅超越直接监督微调，在样本效率上显著优于传统强化学习方法。

Abstract: Real-world agent learning is often constrained by costly environment interactions, such as running time-consuming experiments or obtaining human feedback. In-context learning offers a highly sample-efficient way for agents to learn from their own interaction histories, but its gains disappear once that experience is removed from the context. Separately, context distillation provides a mechanism for internalizing contextual information into model weights. However, applying it to agents' interaction histories without sacrificing environment sample efficiency remains underexplored. We term this problem Experience Distillation and develop an implementation that requires no further environment interaction beyond the collected experience. Experiments on 749 curated software-engineering tasks and six text-adventure games show that it retains at least 64.8\% of the gains from in-context learning across both domains, whereas direct supervised fine-tuning on the collected experience recovers only 3.8\%. Compared with classical reinforcement-learning baselines, in-context learning from trial-and-error experience followed by Experience Distillation matches their performance with at least \(9.6\times\) fewer environment samples.

</details>


### [148] [QuantiBias: Benchmarking Quantization-Induced Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21063)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现大模型量化（压缩）后虽然能通过所有标准安全测试，但其开放式生成中会系统性产生偏见（约24-27%的回答含有刻板印象），且标准评估无法检测到这种偏见。作者提出QuantiBias基准来评估这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化技术被广泛应用于大模型部署，被认为无害且很少重新检查安全性。但作者发现量化的主要副作用是增加了标准安全评估无法捕捉的偏见，这构成了安全隐患。

Method: 提出QuantiBias基准，包含：(1)生成式多语言刻板印象探针；(2)拒绝机制与多选题控制变量；(3)内容严重性评级。在Qwen和Gemma两个骨干模型、五个量化家族、八个基准上进行了系统评估。

Result: 量化模型在所有标准安全检测中表现正常（拒绝有害请求、不过度拒绝良性提示、选择无偏多选题答案），但在开放式问答中约24-27%的回答出现刻板印象。推理（reasoning）对部分量化家族可减半偏见，但对其他家族无效。

Conclusion: 量化模型必须重新评估其开放式生成中的偏见风险，不能仅依靠已有的短形式安全防护。量化器将额外精度分配给能力数据而非偏见预防信号，导致安全问题。

Abstract: Almost every large language model that reaches a broad audience is quantized: trained in full precision, then compressed for efficiency. This step is assumed harmless and its safety is rarely re-checked. We find its principal side effect is increased bias that standard safety evaluation misses. Holding the model, its training, and the prompts fixed, a quantized model still refuses harmful requests, still avoids over-refusing benign prompts, and still selects the unbiased multiple-choice answer. Yet asked an open-ended question, the same model volunteers stereotypes in all eight languages we probe, in roughly one in four open-ended answers under an independent judge (~24% to ~27% across the compression ladder): it passes every standard check and still reaches users measurably more biased. The selective gap is a robust finding; whether open-ended bias further increases with compression is less certain, sensitive to the judge that scores it. We address both with \textbf{QuantiBias}, a benchmark that pairs a generative, multilingual stereotype probe with the refusal and multiple-choice controls that isolate open-ended generation, contrasts each build with and without reasoning, and rates the content severity of what it generates. Across two backbone models (Qwen and Gemma), a five-family screen, and eight benchmarks, quantizers allocate their extra precision by capability data that carries no bias-prevention signal, and reasoning before answering roughly halves the effect on some families while doing nothing on others. A quantized build must be re-evaluated for open-ended bias, not only on the short-form safeguards it already passes.

</details>


### [149] [PrefReward: Learning User Preference Matrix for Personalized Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21067)
*Yue Wu,Chengbing Wang,Yimeng Bai,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PrefReward通过结构化的偏好矩阵显式建模用户风格，并将其作为奖励信号融入解码过程，在LongLaMP数据集上优于非个性化与检索基线，兼顾生成质量与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖模型参数内的隐式表示，难以解读用户特定偏好或有效处理长上下文依赖，亟需一种可解释且高效的个性化生成框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 提取用户特有偏好矩阵，总结个体风格倾向；(2) 通过基于KL散度的奖励函数，将偏好矩阵作为指导信号融入解码过程。

Result: 在LongLaMP数据集上，PrefReward在生成质量和个性化可解释性方面均优于非个性化基线和基于检索的基线。

Conclusion: 显式偏好建模结合奖励信号解码能有效提升LLM个性化生成的可解释性与性能，为处理长上下文依赖提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable ability in generating personalized content by leveraging user histories and contextual cues. However, most existing personalization approaches rely on implicit representations within model parameters, making it difficult to interpret user-specific preferences or effectively handle long-context dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose PrefReward, a novel preference-aware generative framework that explicitly models user styles through a structured preference matrix and integrates it into the decoding process as a reward signal. PrefReward consists of two stages: (1) extracting a user-specific preference matrix that summarizes individual stylistic tendencies, and (2) using the matrix to guide generation via a KL-divergence-based reward function. Experiments on the LongLaMP dataset show that PrefReward outperforms non-personalized and retrieval-based baselines in both generation quality and personalization interpretability.

</details>


### [150] [One More Turn, Less Regret: A Regret-Based Multi-Turn Benchmark for LLMs' Clarification Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21143)
*Minh Ngoc Ta,My Anh Tran Nguyen,Duong D. Nguyen,Yuxia Wang,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RegretBench是一个多轮对话基准，用于评估LLM助手在模糊用户请求下的澄清策略，通过遗憾值（regret）衡量模型相对于参考策略的损失，实验表明仅凭最终成功率无法充分反映澄清效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只评估单轮澄清质量，忽略了澄清本质上是一个序列决策问题——模型需要决定是否提问、问什么、何时停止、何时回答，缺乏对澄清策略行为的多维度评估框架。

Method: 提出RegretBench基准，包含：（1）隐藏意图（hidden-intent）的形式化歧义设定；（2）基于语义状态追踪的自由交互；（3）基于遗憾值（regret）的评估目标，衡量模型相对于参考澄清策略的价值损失。在开放域QA和产品推荐场景下进行实验。

Result: 最终成功率相同的模型在效率、对用户行为的鲁棒性以及停止决策上存在显著差异；有效澄清不仅需要生成合理的问题，还需在正确时机提问并在用户意图明确后及时停止。

Conclusion: RegretBench通过联合衡量意图解析、交互成本、无效澄清和遗憾值，揭示了模型是否进行了有用且高效的澄清，证明了澄清能力评估需要从单轮质量转向多轮策略行为的综合评价。

Abstract: Ambiguous user requests make clarification a sequential decision problem for conversational LLM assistants: they must decide whether to ask, what to ask, when to stop, and when to answer. We introduce RegretBench, a multi-turn benchmark that evaluates clarification as policy behavior rather than isolated question quality. RegretBench provides a hidden-intent formulation of ambiguity, supports free-form interaction grounded in semantic-state tracking, and introduces a regret-based objective that measures how much value a model loses relative to a reference clarification policy. Experiments on open-domain QA and product recommendation scenarios show that final success alone is insufficient, as models with similar accuracy can differ substantially in efficiency, robustness to user behaviors, and stopping decisions. By jointly measuring intent resolution, interaction cost, ineffective clarification, and regret, RegretBench reveals whether models clarify usefully and efficiently. Our results show that effective clarification requires more than plausible questions: models must ask the right question at the right time and stop once the user's intended meaning is clear.

</details>


### [151] [Progressive Cramming: Reliable Token Compression and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21231)
*Dmitrii Tarasov,Timofei Lashukov,Elizaveta Goncharova,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出渐进式压缩方法，通过逐步增加目标前缀token并仅在固定优化预算内无法重建时停止，发现完美重建可通过脆弱导向实现但不具备可迁移语义，因此不足以实现有意义的压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定token预算和99%准确率阈值无法区分残差错误是优化失败还是根本限制，需要更精确的方法来探索压缩极限。

Method: 渐进式压缩逐步增加目标前缀token，在固定优化预算内无法重建时停止；通过因果注意力敲除干预分析嵌入与模型早期层的交互。

Result: 渐进轨迹占据嵌入空间中的低维结构；压缩嵌入导致多项选择基准上适度但一致的准确率下降，在生成式评估下几乎完全崩溃；注意力敲除干预将退化归因于嵌入与模型早期层的交互。

Conclusion: 完美重建（通过脆弱导向而非可迁移语义实现）不足以实现有意义的压缩，渐进式压缩可作为研究压缩极限的工具。

Abstract: Token cramming compresses sequences into learned embeddings with near-perfect reconstruction, but fixed token budgets and 99\% accuracy thresholds leave it unclear whether residual errors reflect optimization failures or fundamental limits. We introduce progressive cramming, which grows the target prefix token-by-token, stopping only when reconstruction is no longer achievable within a fixed optimization budget. Progressive trajectories occupy low-dimensional structure in embedding space. Prepending a crammed embedding causes a moderate but consistent accuracy drop on multiple-choice benchmarks even with the original prefix in context, and collapses capability almost entirely under generative evaluation. Causal attention-knockout interventions trace this degradation to the embedding's interactions in the model's early layers. These results position progressive cramming as a tool for studying compression limits and show that perfect reconstruction - achievable through brittle steering rather than transferable semantics - is insufficient for meaningful compression.

</details>


### [152] [slang.gr as a Large-Scale Crowdsourced Resource for Non-Standard Greek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21255)
*Panagiotis Papadakos,Katerina Papantoniou,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个针对希腊非标准语言的大型计算研究，通过众包词典slang.gr构建结构化多层分类体系，分析俚语的语言结构及社区行为，并提出基于社区的信誉评分机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俚语作为日常语言的重要组成部分，体现了语言创造力、社会身份和文化变迁，但其动态且非标准的特性使得计算建模极具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对非标准希腊语的大规模、系统性计算分析。

Method: 1）将众包的非结构化 folksonomy 标签映射为多层级结构化分类体系，涵盖语义类别和社会语言学元数据；2）基于该表征分析希腊俚语的语言结构和贡献者社区行为；3）提出融合用户角色、交互模式与审核信号的社区置信度评分。

Result: 发现希腊俚语高度集中于与人相关和评价性语言，表现出极强的形态创造力；社区参与高度偏斜，用户生命周期短且存在重叠社群；基于分类体系的表征在保留行为结构信息的同时提升了可解释性。

Conclusion: 将slang.gr确立为非标准希腊语的计算资源，为社会语言学NLP、偏差分析以及LLM中非正式语言研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Slang is a central component of everyday language, reflecting linguistic creativity, social identity, and cultural change, yet its dy- namic and non-standard nature makes it difficult to model computationally. We present the first large-scale computational study of slang.gr, a crowdsourced lexicon of Greek non-standard language, combining lexical content, user-generated tags, and interaction data. To enable the systematic analysis, we map noisy folksonomic tags to a structured multi-layer taxonomy capturing both semantic categories and sociolinguistic metadata. Using this representation, we analyze the linguistic structure of Greek slang and the behavior of its contributor community. We find that slang is strongly centered on person-related and evaluative language, exhibits high morphological creativity, and is shaped by highly skewed participation with short user lifespans and overlapping communities. Building on these signals, we introduce a community-based confidence score for definitions that integrates user roles, interaction patterns, and moderation signals. Our results show that taxonomy-based representations improve interpretability while retaining meaningful aspects of behavioral structure, enabling a more structured and interpretable analysis of confidence signals. Overall, this work establishes slang.gr as a computational resource for non-standard Greek and provides a foundation for sociolinguistic NLP, bias analysis, and the study of informal language in LLMs.

</details>


### [153] [A Comparative Evaluation of Embeddings and LLMs in a Greek Book Publisher Setting - The CUP Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21274)
*Katerina Papantoniou,Panagiotis Papadakos,Theodore Patkos,Dimitris Garefalakis,Nikos Vardakis,Dimitris Plexousakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CUP——一个包含868条目录记录和104条专家标注查询（含分级相关性判断）的希腊语书籍检索基准，评估了稀疏、稠密、混合及LLM辅助检索方法，发现混合检索效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有希腊语信息检索基准匮乏，缺乏能够评估不同检索方法在真实书籍检索场景中表现的数据集，尤其是针对语义、噪声、跨语言查询等复杂场景。

Method: 构建包含868条目录记录和104条专家标注查询的CUP基准，采用分级相关性判断；评估BM25（稀疏）、sentence-transformers（稠密）、混合检索及LLM辅助检索方法；进行查询层面分析，比较命名实体、自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询；探索字段感知提示和LLM目录摘要/后过滤策略。

Result: 多语言嵌入优于专用希腊语模型；混合检索整体表现最佳；BM25在命名实体查询上表现优异，稠密与混合方法在自然语言、噪声、跨语言和概念查询上更好；字段感知提示存在模型特异性影响；LLM目录摘要可提升仅目录检索性能，LLM后过滤提升早期检索但成本高。

Conclusion: CUP基准为希腊语信息检索提供了真实场景评估平台，覆盖词汇、语义、噪声和跨语言查询，为低资源语言的检索系统研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present CUP, a Greek book retrieval benchmark consisting of 868 catalog records and 104 expert-annotated queries with graded relevance judgments. We evaluate sparse (BM25), dense (sentence-transformers), hybrid, and LLM-assisted retrieval methods in this book-search setting. Multilingual embeddings outperform Greek-specific models, while hybrid retrieval performs best overall. A query-level analysis shows that BM25 excels at named-entity queries, while dense and hybrid methods improve natural-language, noisy, cross-lingual, and concept queries. Field-aware prompting has model-specific effects, while LLM TOC summarization improves TOC-only retrieval and LLM post-filtering improves early-stage retrieval at a high cost. Overall, CUP enables real-world evaluation of Greek retrieval across lexical, semantic, noisy, and cross-lingual queries.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Moral-Value Dataset for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21279)
*Zhaohui Zeng,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一的道德价值数据集，可直接用于大语言模型的指令微调，通过将现有道德价值数据集合并并转换为指令-响应格式。实验表明，将通用任务数据集与该数据集混合训练可保持通用任务性能，并初步观察了混合比例对价值导向任务性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调数据集缺乏专门针对道德场景和行为的构建，导致大语言模型在价值对齐（value alignment）方面仍存在挑战。

Method: 将多个现有道德价值数据集合并为统一语料库，并转换为指令-响应格式，构建可直接用于指令微调的统一道德价值数据集。

Result: 在通用任务数据集与道德价值数据集的混合训练下，模型能够保持通用任务性能；混合比例会影响价值导向任务的表现。

Conclusion: 所构建的道德价值数据集可作为进一步对齐研究的有用资源，为LLM价值对齐提供支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have developed rapidly and become valuable tools in everyday life. However, how to align LLMs to a particular set of human values is still an open problem. Recent studies show that instruction tuning has strong potential for zero-shot tasks and may serve as an effective approach to addressing value alignment. Nevertheless, although many datasets for instruction tuning already exist, they are not specifically designed around moral scenarios and behaviors. We construct a unified moral-value dataset that can be directly used for instruction tuning. This dataset is built upon existing moral-value datasets by merging them into a unified corpus and converting them into an instruction-response format. We show that training on a mixed dataset combining general task datasets with our dataset preserves general-task performance, and we report preliminary observations on how the mixing ratio affects value-oriented task performance. Our work provides a moral-value dataset for instruction tuning and offers a useful resource for further alignment research. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/teohzzh/value-for-instruction-tuning.

</details>


### [155] [news-crawler-LM: A Small Long-Context Model For High-Quality News Crawling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21284)
*Pascal Stolzenburg,Jonas Golde,Max Dallabetta,Alan Akbik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了news-crawler-LM，一个基于Fundus新闻爬取库中高质量人工验证数据微调的小型长上下文语言模型，用于从新闻HTML中提取结构化内容。实验表明，该模型在HTML-to-Markdown和HTML-to-JSON任务上优于强基线，但在HTML-to-plaintext任务上仅略优于基于规则的解析库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻网页结构化内容提取方法存在两难：基于规则的爬虫精度高但需手动配置难以泛化，大型语言模型灵活但计算成本高难以实际部署。本文旨在开发一个兼具灵活性与低成本的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 使用Fundus新闻爬取库中高质量、人工验证的提取数据，微调一个小型长上下文语言模型news-crawler-LM，将原始HTML转换为纯文本和结构化JSON（包含标题、作者、发布日期、正文等字段）。

Result: 在HTML-to-Markdown任务上BLEU提升4.8、METEOR提升6.1；在HTML-to-JSON任务上BLEU提升2.2、METEOR提升4.1；在HTML-to-plaintext任务上仅略优于基于规则的解析库。

Conclusion: 小型长上下文语言模型在结构化提取任务上能有效平衡精度与效率，但在纯文本提取任务上优势有限，模型与相关工件已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Extracting structured content from news pages remains challenging due to heterogeneous HTML layouts, inconsistent markup, and substantial boilerplate such as navigation elements and advertisements. Rule-based news crawlers can achieve high extraction accuracy by encoding site-specific structure, but require manual configuration in order to generalize to new publishers. Large language models provide a more flexible alternative by reducing the need for handcrafted rules, but their high computational cost limits practical deployment. In this paper, we introduce news-crawler-LM, a small long-context language model fine-tuned on high-quality, human-validated extractions from the Fundus news-crawling library. Our model converts raw HTML into plaintext and structured JSON, including fields such as headline, author, publication date, and article body. In our experiments, news-crawler-LM outperforms strong baselines in HTML-to-Markdown and HTML-to-JSON extraction, improving performance by +4.8 BLEU and +6.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-Markdown task, and by +2.2 BLEU and +4.1 METEOR in the HTML-to-JSON task. However, we also observe that our model only slightly better compared to other rule-based parsing libraries on the HTML-to-plaintext task in evaluations on previously unseen publishers. We release all models and artifacts to the research community.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework: Similarity-Driven Resource Allocation for Pre-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21291)
*Yidu Wu,Xiang Wang,Kejie Zhao,Zhangchi Wang,Qinghai Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaDSF将预训练LLM转化为深度稀疏模型，基于层间余弦相似度分配token保留比例，通过轻量路由器选择token，并引入特征对齐目标，在减少推理计算量的同时保持性能接近稠密模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构推理成本高，现有加速方法多依赖任务特定微调或从头训练，适应性成本高且跨任务可用性受限。

Method: 提出自适应深度稀疏框架(AdaDSF)：1) 基于层输入输出隐藏状态的余弦相似度统计，分配逐层token保留比例；2) 使用轻量路由器选择每层的信息性token；3) 引入特征保持对齐目标，匹配稀疏与稠密模型之间的中间和最终表示。

Result: 在GPT-NeoX和Qwen2.5的语言建模与常识推理任务上，AdaDSF显著降低推理FLOPs，同时性能接近稠密模型；在同等稀疏度下，准确率退化幅度一致小于MoD、D-LLM和DLO等强基线。

Conclusion: AdaDSF无需完整重新训练即可将预训练LLM转化为深度稀疏模型，有效降低推理成本且保持性能，跨任务泛化性好。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong generation and reasoning performance, but the Transformer architecture incurs high inference cost. Existing acceleration methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or training from scratch, increasing adaptation cost and limiting cross-task usability. We present an Adaptive Depth Sparse Framework (AdaDSF) that converts off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs into depth-sparse models without full retraining. Our key insight is that layers contribute unequally to representation transformation, characterized by the cosine similarity between layer input and output hidden states. Based on this, AdaDSF assigns layer-wise token retention ratios from similarity statistics, uses a lightweight router to select informative tokens at each layer, and introduces a feature-preserving alignment objective to match intermediate and final representations between sparse and dense models. On GPT-NeoX and Qwen2.5 over language modeling and commonsense reasoning, AdaDSF substantially reduces inference FLOPs while preserving performance close to dense counterparts. Under comparable sparsity, AdaDSF consistently yields smaller accuracy degradation than strong baselines including MoD, D-LLM, and DLO.

</details>


### [157] [GRADRAG: Cross-Component Prompt Adaptation for Coordinated Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21324)
*Paolo Pedinotti,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRADRAG是一个跨组件提示适配框架，将RAG流水线建模为计算图，通过结构化评估反馈传播来更新上游代理（检索器、图构造器、回答器），在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，相比仅更新最终生成器的单步优化基线，在LLM评判的成对比较中实现了12-15个百分点的净偏好边际提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统使用多个LLM代理，但大多数工作孤立地优化各个组件，而非协调整个流水线的改进，导致整体性能受限。

Method: 将RAG流水线建模为计算图，通过Evaluator对下游答案和支持证据进行批评并生成可操作的反馈，Prompt Optimizer利用这些反馈迭代更新上游自适应代理（检索器、图构造器、回答器），同时支持在输出满意时提前停止。

Result: 在SQUALITY和QMSUM基准上，在平面块检索（IRCoT风格查询优化）和图检索两种范式下，GRADRAG均优于单步优化基线，LLM评判成对比较中净偏好边际提升12-15个百分点，且多数增益在两次迭代内实现。

Conclusion: 跨组件协同优化比仅优化最终生成器更有效，且收敛速度快（两次迭代内即可获得大部分收益），表明结构化反馈传播机制在RAG系统中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems increasingly employ multiple LLM agents. Yet, most prior work optimizes components in isolation rather than coordinating improvements across the pipeline. We introduce GRADRAG, a framework for cross-component prompt adaptation that models the RAG pipeline as a computational graph and propagates structured evaluation feedback to update upstream agents. An Evaluator critiques downstream answers and supporting evidence, producing actionable feedback that a Prompt Optimizer uses to iteratively update adaptive agents, such as retrievers, graph constructors, and answerers. The Evaluator also triggers early stopping when the output is deemed satisfactory. We evaluate GRADRAG on the SQUALITY and QMSUM benchmarks under two retrieval paradigms: flat chunk-based retrieval using IRCoT-style query refinement (Trivedi et al., 2023), and graph-based retrieval that constructs and iteratively enriches an entity-relation graph from the document. Across both settings, GRADRAG consistently outperforms one-step refinement baselines that update only the final generator, achieving a 12-15 percentage point net preference margin in LLM-judged pairwise comparisons, with most gains realized within two refinement iterations.

</details>


### [158] [Phonetic forced alignment for low-resource language varieties: Model training and evaluation on Chengdu Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21332)
*Zhiheng Qian,Aini Li,Hai Hu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对成都官话这一低资源语言变体，分别训练了基于GMM-HMM的文本相关对齐器（Chengdu-MFA）和基于微调音频编码器的文本无关对齐器（Chengdu-FC），在专家标注测试集上，两者均显著优于标准普通话基线，平均音素边界差异分别降低31.8%和61.2%，建立了无需大量人工标注的低资源语言对齐器引导式开发流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音强制对齐系统缺乏针对低资源语言变体的专用模型，导致在这些变体上对齐精度不足，制约了相关语音学研究。

Method: 使用17小时成都官话语料库和自定义G2P词典，训练了文本相关的GMM-HMM模型（Chengdu-MFA），并利用Chengdu-MFA的伪标签微调预训练音频编码器进行帧分类，得到文本无关对齐器（Chengdu-FC）。

Result: 在专家标注测试集上，Chengdu-MFA平均音素边界差异降低31.8%，Chengdu-FC降低61.2%，均显著优于标准普通话基线。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的引导式开发流程，能够在不依赖耗时人工标注的情况下，为低资源语言变体构建高精度对齐器。

Abstract: Phonetic forced alignment is a key technique in phonetic research, yet existing alignment systems lack specialized models for low-resource language varieties. We address this by training text-dependent and text-independent aligners for Chengdu Mandarin using a 17-hour corpus and a custom G2P dictionary. We trained a text-dependent GMM-HMM model (Chengdu-MFA) and fine-tuned a pretrained audio encoder on frame classification with Chengdu-MFA's pseudo label for text-independent alignment (Chengdu-FC). Evaluation on an expert-annotated test set show that both methods significantly outperform Standard Mandarin baselines. Chengdu-MFA reduced average phone boundary differences by 31.8%, while Chengdu-FC achieved a 61.2% reduction. This work establishes a practical bootstrapping pipeline for developing accurate aligners for under-resourced varieties without labor- and time-intensive manual annotation.

</details>


### [159] [Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score (CM-LRS): From Plausible to Bankable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21340)
*Prerit Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-LRS，一个针对资本市场工作流的大语言模型可靠性评分体系，从事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性等七个维度评估输出，而非仅关注问答层面的准确性。实验表明，前沿闭源模型表现接近（得分4.09-4.31），而开源基线显著落后（3.15），差距主要集中在检索和综合任务上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如开放域QA基准、FinanceBench等）仅评估问答层面的表面准确性，无法衡量模型输出在受监管的资本市场工作流中是否真正"可信"（bankable），即能否在面对交易对手或监管机构时被辩护和采纳。

Method: 提出CM-LRS评分体系，在七个维度（事实准确性、证据可追溯性、数值一致性、工作流完整性、来源纪律、决策有用性、可审查性）上按0-5分制评分，聚合权重可调。在五个资本市场工作流（DCM交易条款提取、先例检索、发行人画像合成、M&A交易可比推理、ECM交易条款提取）上，使用四类独立LLM评委对四个模型进行评分。

Result: 前沿闭源模型（Sonnet 4.6=4.31, Opus 4.7=4.30, GPT-5.5=4.09）在四评委平均CM-LRS上差距仅0.22分；开源基线（Llama 3.3 70B=3.15）显著落后。差距集中于检索（2.23）和综合（2.15）而非提取（0.84）。决策有用性维度跨模型差异最大（发行人画像上达4.0分），且评委间一致性最高（平均r=0.52）。

Conclusion: "似是而非"（plausibility）的生成很容易，但"可信"（bankability）才是真正的门槛。现有模型在资本市场受监管场景下仍存在显著差距，尤其体现在信息检索与综合能力上。

Abstract: In capital-markets workflows the question is rarely whether a large language model can produce a fluent draft, but whether the draft is bankable: defensible in front of a counter-party or a regulator, with the documents in hand. Existing methods address parts of that gap: open-domain QA benchmarks reward surface accuracy, and finance benchmarks (FinanceBench, FinQA, ConvFinQA) advance document-grounded and numerical QA but evaluate at the question-answer layer rather than the workflow outputs practitioners defend.
  We introduce CM-LRS, a Capital Markets LLM Reliability Score, evaluating outputs at the workflow-output layer across seven dimensions: factual accuracy, evidence traceability, numerical consistency, workflow completeness, source discipline, decision usefulness, and reviewability/auditability. Each is scored 0-5 against a rubric anchored on signals reviewers in regulated settings use; the aggregate is tunable to the workflow.
  We demonstrate CM-LRS on five workflows (DCM transaction-terms extraction, precedent retrieval, issuer profile synthesis, M&A transaction-comparable reasoning, ECM transaction-terms extraction) over public SEC EDGAR filings, a public UK takeover release, and fictional synthetic supplements, scoring four models against four independent LLM judges spanning three model families.
  Three findings. First, the frontier closed-source models cluster within 0.22 points on four-judge averaged CM-LRS (Sonnet 4.6 = 4.31, Opus 4.7 = 4.30, GPT-5.5 = 4.09); all four judges place the open-weights baseline (Llama 3.3 70B = 3.15) last. Second, that gap concentrates on retrieval (2.23) and synthesis (2.15), not extraction (0.84). Third, Decision Usefulness shows the widest cross-model dispersion of any dimension (4.0 points on issuer profiling) and top-tier inter-judge agreement (mean r = 0.52).
  Plausibility is cheap. Bankability is the bar.

</details>


### [160] [Word meaning co-determines vowel-inherent spectral change. A corpus-based investigation of conversational Mandarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21391)
*Xiaoyun Jin,Mirjam Ernestus,R. Harald Baayen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自然对话中汉语元音内在频谱变化（VISC），通过广义加性模型和分布语义词嵌入，发现元音共振峰轨迹动态包含与语境意义相关的词特定成分，表明语义共同决定发音的精细细节，挑战了模块化语音产生认知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自然对话语音中元音内在频谱变化是否包含词特定的语义信息，挑战传统模块化认知模型中语音产生与语义分离的观点。

Method: 使用广义加性模型（GAM）控制元音时长、性别、说话人身份、协同发音、元音身份和话语位置等变量，结合分布语义学中的词嵌入（contextualized embeddings）预测F1和F2轨迹。

Result: 词嵌入对F1和F2轨迹的预测准确度显著高于排列基线（permutation baseline），表明元音共振峰轨迹确实包含词特定的、与语境意义相关的成分。

Conclusion: 词汇的语义共同决定了其发音的精细细节，这一发现挑战了语音产生的模块化认知模型。

Abstract: This study investigates vowel-inherent spectral change (VISC) in spontaneous conversational Mandarin. Using the generalized additive model and word embeddings from distributional semantics, we show that, when controlling for variables such as vowel duration, gender, speaker identity, co-articulation, vowel identity, and utterance position, vowel formant trajectory dynamics have word-specific components that are tied to their meaning in context: The F1 and F2 trajectories of words can be predicted from their contextualized embeddings with an accuracy that substantially exceeds a permutation baseline. Challenging modular cognitive models of speech production, these results indicate that, words' semantics co-determine the fine details of their articulation.

</details>


### [161] [MemTools: A Unified Research Framework for Interoperable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21404)
*Chengfeng Zhao,Jinhui Chen,Sirui Liang,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemTools是一个用于Agent记忆系统的互操作性研究框架，通过解耦记忆系统组件与部署环境、标准化记忆生命周期、分离基准数据集与执行协议、提供统一计算接口来协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表征，实现对记忆设计变量的系统隔离与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前记忆系统研究存在架构碎片化问题：不同阶段的记忆生命周期被耦合、评估逻辑与特定数据集纠缠、对异构记忆类型的管理支持有限，这些阻碍了系统化的记忆研究。

Method: MemTools通过声明式数据契约标准化记忆生命周期，实现跨系统组件的可互换组装；正交分离基准数据集与执行协议以支持受控评估；提供统一计算接口在共享运行时中协调符号、神经和多模态记忆表征。

Result: 在跨系统组件集成、评估协议重新配置和异构记忆协调等实证评估中，MemTools能够实现记忆设计变量的系统隔离与分析。

Conclusion: MemTools为推进Agent记忆的 principled 研究提供了实用且可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: While memory systems are essential for agent architectures, pervasive architectural fragmentation restricts systematic research. Existing implementations typically couple different stages of the memory lifecycle, entangle evaluation logic with specific datasets, and provide limited support for the management of heterogeneous memory types. We introduce MemTools, an interoperability research framework that decouples memory system components from their underlying deployment environments. MemTools standardizes the memory lifecycle through declarative data contracts, enabling the interchangeable assembly of components across different systems. It orthogonally separates benchmark datasets from execution protocols to facilitate controlled assessments. Furthermore, MemTools provides a unified computational interface for coordinating symbolic, neural, and multimodal memory representations within a shared runtime. Empirical evaluations on cross-system component integration, evaluation protocol reconfiguration, and heterogeneous memory coordination demonstrate that MemTools enables systematic isolation and analysis of memory design variables. These findings suggest that MemTools provides a practical and extensible infrastructure for advancing principled research on agent memory.

</details>


### [162] [Anti-Periodic Positional Encoding: Möbius Boundary Conditions Make In-Context Retrieval Reliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21405)
*Ji Ho Bae*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Möbius RoPE，一种基于反周期频率梯度的旋转位置编码，通过在每层旋转平面引入奇数倍π的相位推进，使序列两端通过封闭形式的狄拉克“偶极子”确定性耦合，实现反周期边界条件。实验表明，混合模型在保持困惑度不变的同时，显著提升了长上下文检索的可靠性和稳定性，且通过简单的频率表替换即可获得零成本的检索保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转位置编码（RoPE）在长上下文检索中存在不稳定性和随机性（即“种子彩票”问题），导致不同随机种子下检索性能方差极大。作者希望设计一种新的位置编码机制，能够在保持模型困惑度不下降的前提下，提高检索的可靠性和下限，并消除对随机种子的敏感依赖。

Method: 提出 Möbius RoPE，其频率为反周期阶梯 θ_i=π(2i+1)/N，使得每个旋转平面在训练上下文长度上累积奇数倍π的相位，位置和乐为 -1，两端通过狄拉克“偶极子”耦合。在预训练中，将部分注意力头（25%）的频率替换为 Möbius 频率，其余保持标准 RoPE，形成混合模型。

Result: 混合模型困惑度与基线几乎相同（29.66 vs 29.72），但在上下文长度512的单针检索任务中，平均准确率从63.3%提升至90.3%，方差从31.4%降至5.7%，最差种子从14%提升至86%。控制实验证明反周期特性是关键：非周期阶梯无效果，周期阶梯仅部分有效。将训练好的模型频率换回标准 RoPE 会破坏检索能力，且远程检索损伤更大。NoPE 虽短上下文可靠但困惑度高13%且外推最差。

Conclusion: Möbius RoPE 通过反周期边界条件实现了位置编码的确定性长程耦合，在不牺牲语言建模性能的前提下，显著提升了检索的可靠性和稳定性，提供了一种零成本的“检索种子彩票”保险方案。该效应限定于训练窗口内的单针检索任务。

Abstract: Möbius RoPE is a rotary positional encoding built on the anti-periodic frequency ladder $θ_i=π(2i+1)/N$: every rotation plane advances by an odd multiple of $π$ across the training context, so the positional holonomy is $-1$ and the two ends of the sequence are deterministically coupled through a closed-form Dirichlet "dipole"; to our knowledge this is the first anti-periodic boundary condition in positional encoding. We verify the theory numerically to $\sim 10^{-6}$ and pretrain 48 models spanning six 160M-class and three 410M-class arms (2B FineWeb-Edu tokens each; the hybrid arm puts Möbius frequencies on 25% of heads). Hybrid perplexity is unchanged (29.66 vs. 29.72), but needle-in-a-haystack retrieval becomes reliable: $90.3\pm5.7\%$ versus $63.3\pm31.4\%$ at context 512 ($n=6$ seeds), observed worst seed 86% versus 14%, robust variance tests $p=0.013$-$0.029$ (unadjusted), recurring at 410M (Levene $p=0.040$). Matched controls isolate the mechanism: an aperiodic ladder in the same frequency band reproduces none of the effect, and a periodic (holonomy $+1$) ladder only a fraction. Swapping trained models' frequency table back to standard RoPE (weights frozen) collapses retrieval, with damage concentrated on far needles: trained models depend on this long-range geometry. A NoPE arm is even more reliable at short context but pays a 13% perplexity tax and extrapolates worst; only the anti-periodic hybrid pairs baseline perplexity with a high reliability floor. The effect is scoped to single-needle retrieval within the training window; a one-line frequency swap thus provides zero-cost insurance against the retrieval seed lottery.

</details>


### [163] [An Evaluation Framework for Structured Audio Captions Validated by Controlled Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21424)
*Liang-Yuan Wu,Sripathi Sridhar,Mark Cartwright,Magdalena Fuentes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对结构化音频描述的多轴评估框架，结合LLM语义评判与确定性计算指标，通过受控扰动测试验证其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频字幕评估指标仅适用于扁平文本输出，无法可靠评估结构化音频描述中异质的多模态属性。

Method: 在AudioCards数据集基础上，沿标签集、描述、逻辑推理、数值测量、频谱轮廓五个正交轴评估输出；结合LLM评判语义与确定性计算指标测量声学偏差；引入受控扰动测试协议注入分类型、分级别的错误以验证框架可靠性。

Result: 该框架能够有效区分保留语义的改写与真实的语义/声学破坏。

Conclusion: 提出的多轴评估框架填补了结构化音频描述评估的空白，具有区分不同类型和程度错误的能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in automated audio captioning (AAC) have shifted from monolithic sentence generation toward structured formats that explicitly disentangle distinct acoustic and semantic properties. However, evaluating this heterogeneous data remains a significant challenge. Existing caption metrics focus on flat textual outputs and fail to reliably assess multimodal attributes. To bridge this gap, we propose a multi-axis evaluation framework tailored for structured audio descriptions. Building on the AudioCards dataset, we evaluate outputs across five orthogonal axes: tag-sets, descriptions, logical reasoning, numeric measurements, and spectral profiles. Our approach combines Large Language Model (LLM) judges to capture semantic nuance with deterministic computational metrics to precisely measure acoustic deviations. To rigorously validate the reliability of this framework, we introduce a controlled perturbation testing protocol that injects typed, graded errors into groundtruth annotations. Our results demonstrate that this framework successfully distinguishes meaning-preserving paraphrases from genuine semantic and acoustic corruptions.

</details>


### [164] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B的思维链推理存在双峰收敛模式：收敛的生成在AIME上准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，总收敛率62.0%。通过线性探针分析隐藏层激活，层20在token 150处AUC达0.608，显著优于行为基线，但置换检验p=0.063未达常规显著阈值，表明收敛状态在生成结束前已部分编码于中间表征中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究思维链推理模型（DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B）中存在的双峰收敛现象（即生成要么在token预算内终止并给出答案，要么耗尽预算而未得出结论），并验证这一结局是否可以在生成早期通过模型内部表征被检测到，从而为提前退出推理和自适应计算分配提供可能。

Method: 实验方法包括：(1) 在AIME 1983-2024数据集上量化收敛与非收敛生成的准确率差异；(2) 在token位置50-300处提取隐藏状态激活，训练线性探针进行二分类预测；(3) 与基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线进行对比；(4) 使用5折交叉验证和置换检验评估统计显著性。

Result: 收敛生成准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，收敛率62.0%。层20在token 150处AUC达0.608（±0.080），即使在token 50处也可靠高于随机。激活探针一致优于行为基线。置换检验p=0.063（100,000次置换），未达常规显著阈值。

Conclusion: 收敛命运在生成结束前已部分编码于模型中间表征中，但信号较弱，当前样本量不足以在常规显著性水平上确认。该发现为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配开辟了路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [165] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TriviaRoomQA多语言基准，评估30个开源大语言模型在288个主题上的常识与长尾知识。结果表明模型在历史、地理等知识密集型主题上表现强，但在流行文化等日常主题上较弱，且知识获取存在语言依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术基准趋于饱和，无法反映模型在真实场景（如问答节目、 trivia 游戏）中对常识和长尾知识的掌握程度，因此需要构建贴近日常文化、覆盖多语言的知识评估基准。

Method: 构建TriviaRoomQA多语言基准：包含3,300道平行多项选择题（6种欧洲语言）及5,340道法语专用题，覆盖288个主题。评估30个7B-70B参数的开源LLM。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型主题上表现强劲，但在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等流行文化主题上显著较弱；同一问题在不同语言上的表现存在差异，表明知识获取并非完全语言无关。

Conclusion: 当前LLM存在重要的知识缺口——日常流行文化知识不足，且知识访问具有语言依赖性，这一问题未被现有学术饱和基准所捕捉。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个面向俄语长时对话记忆能力的细粒度基准，包含时间戳对话和QA对，支持按语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和显式性维度切片分析。实验揭示了不同记忆机制的优势与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆基准存在英语中心化、仅依赖聚合检索指标、无法捕捉长程上下文与时间推理交互作用的问题。

Method: 构建了包含时间戳的用户-助手对话数据及QA对，提出细粒度分类法涵盖语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达式显式性四个维度，并提供俄语及对齐英语子集。

Result: 评估了当前记忆系统和长上下文模型，展示了RUMBA作为诊断工具按不同切片分析模型行为的能力，识别了不同记忆机制的优缺点和失败模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA为长时对话记忆评估提供了更全面的诊断性基准，弥补了现有基准在语言多样性和多维分析上的不足。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [167] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过2x2实验设计（两种编程语言Python/Rust × 两种模型Qwen2.5-Coder-7B/DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B），系统性研究了独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表示相同概念。结果发现：模型在"哪些概念获得专用电路"上高度一致（通用性体现在表征内容），但在"电路位于何处"和"如何逐层生长"上由模型决定（非通用性体现在计算组织方式）。Rust比Python获得2-3倍更多概念专用电路；两模型间神经元共享程度不同，DeepSeek共享更多；Qwen将Rust类型与特质系统的9个关键字绑定为一个紧密的神经元簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的语言模型在表征和计算组织上是否具有通用性（universality），即不同模型是否以相同方式表示相同概念，从而理解模型内部表征的结构规律。

Method: 采用2×2实验设计：语言维度（Python vs Rust）和模型维度（Qwen2.5-Coder-7B vs DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B），共4个单元格。对每种语言分别提取58个（Python）和57个（Rust）语法概念的概念电路（concept circuit），在全部四个单元格中采用相同的测量方式，分离任务、语言和模型三个因素对电路的影响。

Result: (1) 哪些概念获得电路由任务决定：Python的Spearman ρ=0.638，Rust的ρ=0.673，均p<10⁻⁷；(2) 电路位置由模型决定：Qwen集中在L17-19层，DeepSeek在L6-7层；(3) 电路逐层生长方式由模型决定：Qwen的原子概念有早期峰值而DeepSeek没有；(4) Rust概念获得的概念专用电路比Python多2-3倍；(5) DeepSeek神经元共享程度是Qwen的1.94倍；(6) Qwen将Rust的9个类型/特质关键字绑定为一个紧密神经元簇（Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 电路的通用性（universality）没有单一答案：在"表征内容"（What）上是通用的，但在"计算组织"（Where和How）上不是通用的。通用性是关于表征内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。所有结论限定于本次2×2设计，下一步将检验模型侧的分布模式能否预测第三个模型。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [168] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型系统性地过度使用古修辞格"epanorthosis"（自我修正），这主要源于训练数据中的促销性文本和RLHF偏好调优。通过建立Epanorthosis Index（EI）测量，发现模型在演讲类文本中过度使用（意大利语中接近三倍），在非正式问答中使用不足，而论证、新闻和百科类文本与人类相当。研究展示了LoRA适配器、指令微调等方法可将该修辞格频率校准至人类水平，并强调目标应是校准而非消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大语言模型中一种被忽视的系统性修辞偏差——epanorthosis（自我修正）的过度使用，并论证这种过度使用是训练数据分布和RLHF偏好调优共同塑造的"训练性倾向"，而非简单的自回归生成副作用。

Method: 提出Epanorthosis Index（EI），即该修辞格密度相对于人类基准率的比值；在三种规模的指令调优模型上，对多种体裁（演讲、非正式问答、论证、新闻、百科）进行测量；对比意大利语和英语的跨语言表现；探索LoRA适配器、单行指令、SFT适配器加缩放系数等缓解技术。

Result: 模型在演讲类文本中过度使用epanorthosis（约两倍，意大利语接近三倍），在非正式问答中不足使用，在论证、新闻和百科类文本中与人类匹配。单行指令可将该修辞格减少一半至近四分之三，SFT适配器几乎完全消除，缩放系数可精确调回人类水平。

Conclusion: 目标不是消除模型的修辞特征，而是按体裁校准至人类使用率。真正的风险不在于模型像人一样写作，而在于人开始像机器一样写作。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [169] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO是一个面向非洲语言的开放自动语音识别基础模型家族，基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器构建，包含21个单语模型和5个多语模型，覆盖加纳、塞拉利昂、尼日利亚、塞内加尔、肯尼亚和津巴布韦的27种语言变体。模型在宗教文本朗读语音上微调，采用两到三步的学习率退火微调策略，并引入轻量级语言条件机制。多语模型平均词错误率(WER)达到10-13%，所有模型在Apache-2.0许可下发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏充足的标注语音数据和开放许可的ASR模型，现有资源主要面向高资源语言。DONDO旨在通过利用宗教文本（提供广泛、许可清晰且拼写一致的覆盖）来填补这一空白，为非洲低资源语言构建高质量、开放许可的ASR基础模型。

Method: 基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器，在宗教文本朗读语音上微调。采用两到三步的学习率退火微调策略：先用高学习率适配共享的多语模型，再通过退火恢复（部分超越）强单语基线。引入轻量级语言条件机制，将one-hot语言身份作为前缀帧注入声学特征，使单个多语检查点在推理时可导向目标语言。

Result: 21个单语模型和5个多语模型，覆盖27种语言变体。五个多语家族的退火模型平均WER达到10-13%，基本缩小了与单语模型的差距，同时用单个检查点覆盖多种语言。覆盖语言约有1亿第一语言使用者，加上第二语言使用者则更多。

Conclusion: DONDO展示了使用宗教文本作为低资源ASR训练数据源的可行性，验证了学习率退火多语微调策略和轻量级语言条件机制的有效性。所有模型在Apache-2.0许可下开源（Hugging Face KhayaAI），允许包括商业用途在内的自由微调，为非洲语言ASR研究提供了重要基础资源。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [170] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedGame是一个将静态临床病例转化为结构化、可执行的叙事游戏框架，采用双引擎设计（医学叙事设计器和故事导演），构建了5000病例的MedGame Bench基准，实验表明微调可提升开源模型性能，学生认为MedGame比纯文本方案更具吸引力和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在医学教育中的应用多局限于局部交互（如问答或单轮反馈），未能将完整临床病例组织成以决策为中心的学习轨迹，缺乏系统性叙事框架。

Method: 提出MedGame框架，包含Medical Narrative Designer（合成基于病例的临床故事情节，含状态和决策节点）和Story Director（将叙事情节转化为依赖感知的多模态编排计划），并由交互式平台渲染执行。构建MedGame Bench基准（5000病例）用于医学叙事生成和故事导向评估。

Result: 任务特定微调显著提升了开源LLM在MedGame Bench上的表现，缩小了与商业模型的差距；学生试点研究显示学习者认为MedGame比纯文本方案更具参与感和实用性。

Conclusion: MedGame通过叙事游戏化框架有效增强了医学教育中的病例学习体验，为LLM在医学教育中的系统化应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [171] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了惊讶度理论（surprisal theory）在无额外约束条件下是一个同义反复（tautology）：任何非负的难度度量都可以找到某个语言模型使其惊讶度与之成仿射关系。过去二十年的研究隐含假设相关语言模型是训练语料的生成分布，但近期实证表明更好的语料模型反而可能是更差的认知预测器。打破这一同义反复需要理性主义干预，即语言模型应源自非经验驱动的理解者模型（如基于记忆约束或处理目标）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出惊讶度理论在缺乏额外约束时是空洞的同义反复，无法做出可证伪的预测，从而挑战该理论的核心假设，并推动心理语言学方法论上的反思。

Method: 通过理论论证（任何非负难度度量都存在对应语言模型使其惊讶度与之仿射相关）结合对已有实证研究的分析（更好的语料模型反而更差地预测人类处理难度），揭示该理论的逻辑缺陷。

Result: 证明惊讶度理论在无约束条件下无法被证伪；发现"语料生成分布"这一隐含假设已被实证研究否定。

Conclusion: 打破同义反复需要理性主义方法：语言模型应来自非经验性的理解者模型（如记忆约束或处理目标），而非依赖惊讶度理论试图解释的行为数据。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Pipelined Gradient Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20739)
*Xian Su,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出流水线梯度编码（Pipelined GC），将梯度评估分步进行，使每个工作节点每步仅评估单个数据分区，相比传统梯度编码（需每步评估多个分区）显著减少训练时间并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度编码（GC）要求工作节点每步评估多个数据分区的梯度，这反而可能增加整体训练时间；此外，掉队者（stragglers）问题会严重拖慢分布式训练速度。

Method: 提出流水线梯度编码方法，将梯度评估过程分段跨多个步骤执行，每个工作节点每步仅处理单个数据分区。具体针对分数重复（FR）和循环重复（CR）两种代表性数据放置方案设计了流水线版本，并给出了收敛性证明。

Result: 通过大量仿真和云基础设施实验表明，所提方案相比传统梯度编码及其他基线方法，不仅显著减少了训练时间，还加速了收敛过程。

Conclusion: 流水线梯度编码能有效缓解掉队者问题，在保持收敛性保证的同时降低每步计算负担，是分布式训练中一种更高效的梯度编码方案。

Abstract: In large-scale machine learning, distributed training commonly involves multiple workers evaluating the gradients of the model on different dataset partitions. A common challenge is the presence of straggling workers, which may significantly slow down training. Traditional gradient coding (GC) addresses this by duplicating dataset partitions across workers, allowing for the replacement of missing gradients from stragglers. However, GC requires workers to evaluate gradients on multiple dataset partitions in each step, potentially increasing overall training time. In this paper, we propose to pipeline GC, such that gradient evaluation is segmented across multiple steps and each worker evaluates gradients on just a single dataset partition per step. We develop the pipelined version for fractional repetition (FR) and cyclic repetition (CR), two representative dataset placement schemes in GC, and prove convergence guarantees for both. Through extensive simulations and experiments on cloud infrastructure, our schemes not only significantly reduce training time but also accelerate convergence compared to GC and other baselines.

</details>


### [173] [New Criteria and Constructions for Self-Orthogonal Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20805)
*Peng Wang,Ziling Heng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过建立新的自正交性判据，利用定义集构造、乘法子群作用以及特征函数方法，构造了多类新的自正交码和自正交极小码，并由此导出了最优或接近最优的量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自正交码在量子纠错码、线性互补对偶码等领域有重要应用，因此构造新的自正交码族具有理论和实际价值。

Method: 1) 对q>3，证明当定义集D是G-不变时（G⊆𝔽_q^*且|G|>2），码𝒞_D是自正交的；对q=2,3，用特征和刻画自正交性；结合部分差集构造新族。2) 利用乘法子群G作用在射影线性码的列上构造参数灵活的自正交码，并通过增广码导出量子码。3) 采用特征函数法构造线性码并建立自正交性判据，利用向量对偶bent函数和s- plateaued函数构造违反Ashikhmin-Barg条件的自正交极小码。

Result: 构造了多类新的自正交码族，得到了最优或接近最优的量子码，以及违反Ashikhmin-Barg条件的自正交极小码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多种构造方法有效丰富了自正交码和自正交极小码的族类，并为量子码的构造提供了新途径。

Abstract: Self-orthogonal codes have attracted considerable attention owing to their applications in quantum error-correcting codes, linear complementary dual codes, and a variety of other fields.
  In this paper, we construct new families of self-orthogonal codes and self-orthogonal minimal codes by establishing criteria that characterize the self-orthogonality of certain linear codes. To this end, we first establish several new criteria for linear codes arising from the defining-set construction to be self-orthogonal. More specifically, for $q > 3$, we show that the code $\mathcal{C}_D$ is self-orthogonal whenever the defining set $D$ is $G$-invariant, where $G \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^*$ and $|G| > 2$. For $q = 2, 3$, we characterize the self-orthogonality of $\mathcal{C}_D$ using certain character sums. By combining these criteria with partial difference sets, we construct several new families of self-orthogonal codes, which lead to optimal or almost optimal quantum codes. Secondly, via the action of a multiplicative subgroup $G \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^*$ with $|G| > 2$ on the columns of a projective linear code, we construct self-orthogonal codes with flexible parameters. From their augmented codes, we derive quantum codes. By choosing suitable projective codes, we obtain optimal quantum codes with high parametric flexibility. Thirdly, we employ the characteristic function method to construct linear codes and establish criterion for their self-orthogonality. Based on this, we construct several classes of self-orthogonal minimal codes that violate the Ashikhmin-Barg condition, using vectorial dual-bent functions and $s$-plateaued functions.

</details>


### [174] [Weak Private Information Retrieval for Graph-based Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21014)
*Shodasakshari Vidya,Chandan Anand,Prasad Krishnan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文正式提出了基于图的弱私密信息检索（G-WPIR）概念，在放宽完美隐私约束的条件下，通过最小子包化和简单概率查询实现，在互信息泄漏和最大泄漏两种度量下，为任意图及完全图、完全二分图等特殊图类建立了速率-隐私权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的基于图复制存储系统的私密信息检索（G-PIR）研究均假设完美信息论隐私（零泄漏），但这一约束过于严格，限制了检索速率的上限。本文旨在通过适当放松隐私约束，探索能否获得更高的检索速率，从而填补弱隐私（非零泄漏）场景下基于图的PIR研究的空白。

Method: 提出一种适用于任意图的G-WPIR方案，采用最小子包化技术（表示文件大小约束）和简单的概率查询实现机制，在互信息泄漏和最大泄漏两种度量下分析速率-隐私权衡。并针对完全图和完全二分图两类特殊图结构，对方案进行修改和优化。

Result: 在任意图上实现了G-WPIR方案，建立了速率与隐私之间的平滑权衡关系；针对完全图和完全二分图，给出了改进方案及对应的速率-隐私权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地研究了基于图的弱私密信息检索问题，证明了通过适当放松隐私约束可以显著提升检索速率，为实际分布式存储系统中隐私与效率的权衡设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A distributed storage system with graph-based replication consists of a collection of databases and the files they contain. The databases (or servers) are represented as the vertices of a graph, while each file is stored in a distinct pair of servers and is represented by an edge of this graph. Private information retrieval (G-PIR) on such a graph-based storage system involves a client which seeks to retrieve a desired file via a query-response protocol, without leaking the identity of the desired file index to any database. The goal of G-PIR is to maximize the rate (reciprocal of the total normalized download) under the privacy constraint. Prior work on G-PIR has involved perfect information-theoretic privacy (i.e., null leakage). However, if the privacy constraint is relaxed, then PIR protocols could be designed that have even higher rates. We term such protocols as Graph-based Weak Private Information Retrieval (G-WPIR) protocols and initiate their formal study in this work. We propose a G-WPIR scheme for arbitrary graphs, and identify the trade-offs it achieves between rate and privacy, under two well known leakage metrics: mutual information leakage and maximal leakage. Our protocol employs minimal subpacketization (representing a file-size constraint) and employs a simple probabilistic query realization to obtain the smooth trade-off. We extend this protocol with some modifications to two special classes of graphs, the complete graphs and the complete bipartite graphs, and identify the corresponding rate-privacy trade-offs achieved.

</details>


### [175] [Construction and Dynamic Update of Channel Gain Maps via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21099)
*Yilong Chen,Yuan Guo,Juncong Zhou,Jie Xu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维高斯溅射（3DGS）的物理信息网格信道增益图（CGM）构建与动态更新方法，通过将传播环境表示为高斯原语集合并引入增量学习机制，实现了大规模网络信道图的高效重构与环境自适应更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道知识地图（CKM）是实现环境感知无线网络设计的关键技术，但现有方法在大规模网络中难以高效构建和实时更新网格级信道增益图（CGM），尤其面临相位敏感小尺度衰落波动和动态环境变化带来的挑战。

Method: 首先建立基于网格的信道增益模型，将接收端网格上的本地平均信道增益分解为距离相关衰减、路径透射率和有效散射贡献三个物理因子；然后提出物理信息高斯溅射信道增益（GS-CG）模型，将传播环境表示为具有几何、不透明度和方向特征的高斯原语，通过可微渲染过程生成网格级信道增益；最后引入增量学习机制，冻结参考高斯原语并引入紧凑可调高斯原语，利用稀疏测量捕捉环境变化引起的局部信道增益变化。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提GS-CG方法能够精确重构基于网格的CGM，有效适应动态环境变化，并在快速CGM优化中实现了良好的精度-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 基于3DGS的物理信息模型结合增量学习机制，为大规模网络中的信道增益图构建与动态更新提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，在精度与计算效率之间取得了有利平衡。

Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) have emerged as a promising technique for providing scene-specific and location-dependent propagation knowledge to enable environment-aware wireless network design. This paper investigates the construction and dynamic updating of a particular type of CKM, namely grid-based channel gain maps (CGMs), for large-scale networks using three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS). First, we formulate a grid-based channel gain model, where each map entry is defined as the locally averaged channel gain over a receiver grid, thereby suppressing phase-sensitive small-scale fluctuations. The resulting channel gain is decomposed into distance-dependent attenuation, path transmittance, and effective scattering contributions. Based on this decomposition, we develop a physics-informed Gaussian-splatting-based channel gain (GS-CG) model, which represents the propagation environment as a set of Gaussian primitives. The proposed model maps Gaussian geometry, opacity, and directional features to propagation-related factors and renders grid-level channel gains through a differentiable process. To accommodate real-time environmental changes, we further propose an incremental learning mechanism that updates a static reference GS-CG representation into a dynamic CGM. Specifically, the reference Gaussian primitives are frozen, while a compact set of tunable Gaussians is introduced to capture newly induced local channel-gain variations from sparse measurements.Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed GS-CG methods accurately reconstruct grid-based CGMs, efficiently adapt to dynamic environmental changes, and achieve a favorable accuracy-complexity tradeoff for fast CGM refinement.

</details>


### [176] [Electromagnetic-Aware Fluid Antenna Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21375)
*Zhentian Zhang,Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zhen Chen,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种电磁感知的电流域框架用于平面流体天线阵列（FAA），该框架统一建模了位置相关的多端口阻抗、互耦、辐射与接收功率、源电压可行性及信道变化，并基于此设计了两种优化问题：单波束超方向性波束赋形（联合优化端口电流与位置）和多用户加权和速率最大化（电流域预编码与位置优化）。仿真表明，合理建模的互耦可作为有价值的设计资源，在固定网格和随机流体天线基准上实现更低的旁瓣和持续的速率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数通信模型将天线端口视为独立信道样本，忽略了电磁耦合对紧凑孔径的根本性影响，导致无法准确描述平面FAA的电磁行为。

Method: 建立了电磁感知的电流域框架，包含位置依赖的多端口阻抗、互耦、辐射/接收功率、源电压可行性和信道变化，形成统一基带兼容描述。框架实现无关，可采用半波偶极子闭式模型（本文实例）或全波、测量、代理阻抗及嵌入方向图模型。在此基础上提出两种优化问题：1) 单波束超方向性波束赋形（联合优化端口电流与位置，含旁瓣/电流/电压/几何约束）；2) 多用户加权和速率最大化（电流域预编码与位置优化，含接收功率/电流/电压/间距约束）。采用交替算法，电流/预编码子问题为凸优化，几何更新为简化梯度法。

Result: 当正确建模互耦时，可将其作为有价值的设计资源加以利用，相比固定网格和随机流体天线基准，能实现更低的旁瓣和持续的速率增益。

Conclusion: 电磁耦合不应被视为需要纠正的缺陷，而是可通过联合优化电流与位置加以利用的设计资源，所提框架为实际FAA系统提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays (FAAs) offer a promising means of exploiting spatial degrees of freedom through adaptive port positioning. However, most existing communication models treat antenna ports as independent channel samples and therefore overlook the electromagnetic coupling that fundamentally governs compact apertures. This paper develops an electromagnetic-aware current-domain framework for planar FAAs. The proposed model integrates position-dependent multiport impedance, mutual coupling, radiated and accepted power, source-voltage feasibility, and channel variations into a unified baseband-compatible description. The framework is implementation-agnostic: the closed-form half-wave-dipole model adopted in this paper is only one instance and can be replaced by full-wave, measured, or surrogate impedance and embedded-pattern models. Building on this framework, we formulate two optimization-oriented design problems. The first addresses single-beam superdirective beamforming through the joint optimization of port currents and positions under sidelobe, current, voltage, and geometry constraints. The second maximizes the multi-user weighted sum rate via current-domain precoding and position optimization under accepted-power, current, voltage, and spacing constraints. In both cases, the electromagnetic model is not applied as an after-design correction, but is incorporated directly into tractable alternating algorithms with convex current or precoding subproblems and reduced-gradient geometry updates. Simulation results demonstrate that, when properly modeled, mutual coupling can be exploited as a valuable design resource, enabling lower sidelobes and persistent sum-rate gains over fixed-grid and random fluid-antenna benchmarks.

</details>


### [177] [Improved lower bounds for the Shannon capacity of odd cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21517)
*Nathaniel Itty,Christopher D. Rosin,Chase Carstensen,Daniel Reichman*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过LLM交互发现了奇圈强幂的大独立集，改进了C7、C11、C13的Shannon容量下界，分别达到3.258020、5.289773和6.300109。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon容量是图论中衡量无错信道传输速率的重要参数，其下界可通过计算奇圈强幂的独立集来改进。现有下界仍有提升空间，需要构造更大的独立集。

Method: 通过与大型语言模型（LLM）迭代交互，辅助发现显式组合构造，从而构建出奇圈强幂中更大的独立集。

Result: 在C_7^{10}中构造了134753大小的独立集，将Θ(C_7)下界提升至3.258020；在C_11^{6}中构造了21909大小的独立集，将Θ(C_11)下界提升至5.289773；在C_13^{6}中构造了62530大小的独立集，将Θ(C_13)下界提升至6.300109。此外还改进了多个奇圈单个强幂的独立数下界。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效辅助发现显式组合构造，提供了改进Shannon容量下界的新途径。

Abstract: The Shannon capacity $Θ(G)$ of a graph $G$ quantifies the maximum rate at which information can be transmitted with zero error over a noisy channel. It is lower bounded by $α(G^d)^{1/d}$ for any $d$, where $α(G^d)$ is the independence number of the $d$-th strong power of $G$. We construct independent sets of size $134753$ in $C_7^{10}$, $21909$ in $C_{11}^{6}$, and $62530$ in $C_{13}^{6}$, improving the best known lower bounds for the Shannon capacity of these graphs to $Θ(C_7)\geq 134753^{1/10}>3.258020$, $Θ(C_{11})\geq 21909^{1/6}>5.289773$, and $Θ(C_{13})\geq 62530^{1/6}>6.300109$. We also improve the best known lower bounds on the independence numbers of several individual strong powers of odd cycles that do not improve the Shannon capacity lower bound. The constructions were discovered through iterative interactions with a Large Language Model (LLM), illustrating the potential of LLMs for finding explicit combinatorial constructions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [A generalized shape function approach for multimaterial topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20784)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Nikhil Singh,Anupam Saxena,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义形状函数（gSF）方法，用于多材料拓扑优化。该方法利用紧致设计空间，通过n维（n-线性）形状函数将多材料单纯形域映射到设计变量，并证明了密度满足重心坐标性质。通过密度滤波和投影方案保持关键属性，在2D和3D的刚架结构（SS）和柔性机构（CM）设计问题中验证了其有效性，支持多达24种（2D）和15种（3D）材料，具有良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑优化方法在处理大量候选材料时存在实际限制，缺乏良好的可扩展性。本文旨在提出一种能够直接处理任意多种材料、且保证物理有效性的通用方法。

Method: 基于1D、2D和3D形状函数推广到广义nD（n-线性）形状函数，将多材料单纯形域映射到设计变量。设计变量为自然坐标，通过数学证明其密度满足重心坐标性质。采用密度滤波直接作用于自然坐标，并设计投影方案以保持重心坐标性质。灵敏度分析使用伴随变量法，优化求解采用移动渐近线法（MMA）。

Result: 在2D和3D的刚架结构和柔性机构设计问题中验证了方法有效性。SS和CM问题分别展示了多达24种（2D）和15种（3D）不同材料的结果。目标函数历史曲线显示平滑收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的gSF方法消除了候选材料数量的实际限制，具有良好的可扩展性，适用于大规模工程应用中的多材料拓扑优化。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized shape function (gSF) approach for multi-material topology optimization that utilizes a compact design space to produce optimized configurations featuring a large number of materials. Building upon 1D (linear), 2D (bilinear), and 3D (trilinear) shape functions, generalized nD (n-linear) shape functions are conceptualized to map the multi-material simplex domain. Natural coordinates of these shape functions are considered the design variables used to determine the material densities. These densities are mathematically proven to satisfy the essential barycentric properties, guaranteeing a physically valid material interpolation space. Furthermore, we demonstrate that applying density filtering directly to the natural coordinates is mathematically equivalent to filtering the densities themselves, and that the tailored projection scheme preserves these vital barycentric properties in the projected states. The versatility, efficacy, and success of the gSF approach are demonstrated across various 2D and 3D stiff-structure (SS) and compliant-mechanism (CM) design problems. Strain energy is minimized for SS, whereas a multicriteria objective is minimized for CMs with given volume constraints. Sensitivity analysis is performed using the adjoint variable method, and the optimization problem is solved using the method of moving asymptotes. Results for SSs and CMs in 2D and 3D, respectively, up to 24 and 15 different materials, are presented. Objective history plots indicate smooth convergence. The proposed approach removes practical restrictions on the number of candidate materials, offering excellent scalability for large-scale engineering applications.

</details>


### [179] [Chemical Chain-of-Thought Functions as a Hallucination-Prone Molecular Scratchpad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20935)
*Jiatong Li,Yuxuan Ren,Weida Wang,Xiaoyong Wei,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 化学推理语言模型在链式思维推理中普遍存在幻觉（虚构分子结构），但正确回答常与幻觉共存；推理轨迹不是忠实解释也不是事后合理化，而是易产生幻觉的分子草稿本，其结构草稿对生成结果有因果性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究化学推理语言模型在链式思维推理中是否真正具备忠实推理能力，验证CoT中结构声明的准确性与对最终答案的因果作用。

Method: 对四种推理模型家族（Chem-R、ether-0、ChemDFM-R等）在十二个化学任务上进行系统分析，通过归因分析、扰动实验（干扰SMILES草稿）等方法评估CoT中结构幻觉的程度及其与答案正确性的关系。

Result: 幻觉普遍存在且与答案正确性解耦；不同模型使用不同形式的草稿本（Chem-R/ether-0用SMILES片段，ChemDFM-R用骨架、位置和命名线索）；扰动SMILES草稿会降低生成质量，证明结构草稿具有因果作用。

Conclusion: 化学CoT既非忠实解释也非事后合理化，而是易产生幻觉的分子草稿本；不应将CoT视为忠实推理的直接证据，需要超越仅答案评估的过程级监督。

Abstract: Chemical reasoning language models are expected to derive molecular answers through faithful chain-of-thought (CoT). However, across four reasoning model families and twelve chemistry tasks, hallucination is widespread and largely decoupled from answer correctness: correct answers often coexist with fabricated structural claims absent from the relevant molecules. Yet this does not make the reasoning trace computationally irrelevant. Attribution analyses suggest a shared scratchpad function expressed in model-specific forms: Chem-R and ether-0 rely on fragmented SMILES drafts, whereas ChemDFM-R emphasizes scaffold, positional, and naming cues. Notably, perturbing Chem-R's SMILES sketches degrades generation, showing that structural drafts can be causally load-bearing even when verbal structural claims are largely inert. Together, these results show that chemical CoT is neither a faithful explanation nor merely a post-hoc rationalization, but a hallucination-prone molecular scratchpad. This finding cautions against treating CoT as direct evidence of faithful reasoning and motivates process-level supervision beyond answer-only evaluation.

</details>


### [180] [EuroFlood: a Python library and queryable index for the CEMS satellite-derived flood-depth archive of Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21126)
*Jürgen Hackl*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文介绍EuroFlood，一个开源Python库和空间索引，用于高效查询和检索欧洲应急管理服务（Copernicus EMS）的洪水水深栅格数据，支持洪水模型验证和影响评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Copernicus EMS的洪水水深数据以数千个栅格文件形式分发，唯一空间元数据仅为文件名中的坐标，导致数据被发现和查询极为困难，缺乏可查询的空间索引来支持大规模洪水分析。

Method: 开发EuroFlood开源Python库，构建空间索引记录每个栅格单元被哪些洪水事件淹没，从而实现事件发现、复发频率和足迹查询仅需处理档案中极小部分数据；水深栅格按需检索；索引能无损失复现档案中的事件足迹。

Result: 索引能够无损失复现存档事件足迹；与独立洪水影响数据库的完整性审计表明大多数记录的洪水与存档事件共现；展示了大陆尺度的洪水复发映射、观测与模拟洪泛区对比以及基于事件的暴露评估。

Conclusion: EuroFlood使原本难以查询的大型洪水水深档案变得可高效检索，支持洪水模型验证、影响评估等多类应用，为大陆尺度洪水分析提供了重要的基础设施。

Abstract: Satellite-derived observations of flood water depth support flood model validation and impact assessment, yet the only open continental-scale archive of such observations, the flood-depth maps of the Copernicus Emergency Management Service, is distributed as several thousand raster files whose sole spatial metadata is a coordinate encoded in each filename. This paper presents EuroFlood, an open-source Python library and a published spatial index that make the archive queryable. The index records which events inundated each grid cell, so discovery, recurrence, and footprint queries are answered from a small fraction of the archive volume, while depth rasters are retrieved on demand. It reproduces the archived event footprints losslessly at its grid, and a completeness audit against an independent flood-impact database shows that most documented floods co-occur with archived events. Demonstrations include continental recurrence mapping, comparison of observed with modelled flood extents, and event-based exposure assessment.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Finding Fast Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20634)
*Karima Ma,Andrew Adams,Jonathan Ragan-Kelley*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一多种快速FIR滤波器近似技术的设计语言，通过自动搜索程序空间并结合梯度下降拟合连续参数，生成性能与质量之间的帕累托前沿，最终自动编译为优化的C++代码，在图像和音频滤波任务上取得比以往更优的效果。
